apiVersion: ncm.nokia.com/v1alpha1
kind: NextHopGroup
metadata:
labels:
controller-tools.k8s.io: "1.0"
name: nexthopgroup-external
spec:
nodeSelector:
is_edge:"true" # Nodes providing gateway/egress function.
nextHopTemplate:
addressFamily: IPv4 # Route address family. It must be the same as the one in
EgressGateway. Options: IPv4, IPv6.
bidirectionalForwardingDetectionInterface: vlan201 # Interface as BFD local peer on gateway node,
gateway for worker nodes.
sourceNetworkAddressTranslation: true # Enable SNAT for outgoing traffic
sourceNetworkAddressTranslationInterfaces: # Interfaces on which SNAT is performed. Usually,
these are interfaces via which traffic goes to the external.
- vlan220

-bash-4.4# cat NextHopGroup.yaml
apiVersion: ncm.nokia.com/v1alpha1
kind: NextHopGroup
metadata:
  labels:
    controller-tools.k8s.io: "1.0"
  name: nexthopgroup-external
spec:
  nodeSelector:
    is_edge: "true"
  nextHopTemplate:
    addressFamily: IPv4
    bidirectionalForwardingDetectionInterface: vlan201
    sourceNetworkAddressTranslation: true
    sourceNetworkAddressTranslationInterfaces:
      - vlan220
-bash-4.4# cat EgressGateway.yaml
apiVersion: ncm.nokia.com/v1alpha1
kind: EgressGateway
metadata:
  name: egressgateway-external
spec:
  nodeSelector:
    is_worker: "true"
  nodeEgressGatewayTemplate:
    addressFamily: IPv4
    interface: vlan201
    nextHopGroup: nexthopgroup-external
